1b1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/bam po nyis brgya 
1b2('bum): brgyad pa/rab 'byor gzhan yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di ni bar chad du 'gyur ba mang 
1b3('bum): ba ste/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa zab mo 'di 'dri ba dang /
1b4('bum): klog pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du 
1b5('bum): yid la byed pa de dag la yang bar chad du 'gyur ba mang//rab 'byor 'di lta ste/dper na/
1b6('bum): 'gro ba'i gnyen gcig gtsug tor can 
2a1('bum): @##//'dzam bu'i gling 'di'i rin po che gser dang /nor bu dang /mu tig dang /bai DU r+ya dang /dung 
2a2('bum): dang /man shel dang /byi ru dang /dngul dang /sa le sbram gang ji snyed cig yod pa de dag ni bar chad du 'gyur 
2a3('bum): ba mang zhing phyir rgol ba mang ba dag go/rab 'byor de bzhin du rigs kyi bu'am/rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi 
2a4('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di 'dri ba dang /glog pa dang /lung 'bogs pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston 
2a5('bum): pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa de dag la yang bdud kyi las mang po 'byung bar 'gyur ro//bar chad mang 
2b1('bum): po 'byung bar 'gyur ro//phyir rgol ba mang po 'byung bar 'gyur ro/rab 'byor gyis gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das de ni de bzhin no//bad ba rag shegs pa de ni de bzhin te/'dzam bu'i 
2b2('bum): gling 'di'i rin po che'i gser dang /nor bu dang /mu tig dang /baiDUr+ya dang /dung dang /man shel dang /byi ru dang /dngul dang /sa le sbram gang ji snyed cig mchis pa de dag ni bar chad du 'gyur 
2b3('bum): ba mang zhing slar rgol ba mang ba lags so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di 'dri ba dang /klog pa dang /lung 'bogs 
2b4('bum): pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du yang la bgyid pa de dag la yang bdud kyi las mang po 'byung bar 'gyur re//bar chad mang po 'byung bar 'gyur ra//slar rgol ba mang po 
2b5('bum): 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das skyes bu glen pa gang rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo shes rag kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zag mo 'di 'dri ba dang //klog pa dang /lung 
2b6('bum): 'bogs pa dang /kha ton byed ba dang /ston pa dang /chul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa dag gi bar chad bgyid pa de ni bdud kyi byin gyis brlabs pa ste/bcom ldan 'das skyes pu glen pa de dag gi 
2b7('bum): blo chung zhing blo zhan par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das skyes bu gang rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di 'dri ba 'am/klog pa dang /lung 'bogs pa 
2b8('bum): dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid ba de dag gi bar chad bgyid ba de dag ni blo rgya shin tu chung bar 'gyur te/sangs rgyas kyi chos rlabs po che dag la de dag gi 
3a1('bum): @#//sems mi 'jug go/de dag ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di mi 'dri mi klog/lung mi 'bog/kha ton mi bgyid/mi sgom tshul bzhin du yid la mi bgyid de/gzhan 
3a2('bum): 'dri zhing klog pa dang /kha ton bgyid pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa dag la yang bar chad bgyid par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor 
3a3('bum): de de bzhin no//de de bzhin te/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa zab mo 'di mi 'dri/mi klog/lung mi 'bogkha ton mi byed/mi sgom/tshul bzhin 
3a4('bum): du yid la mi byed cing /gzhan 'dri ba dang /klog pa dang /lung 'bogs pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang/tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa dag gi bar chad byed pa de dag ni/bdud 
3a5('bum): kyi byin gyis brlabs pa/theg pa la gsar du zhugs pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba shin tu chung ba/sngon gyi rgyal ba la dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bsgrubs pa/dge ba'i bshes    
3a6('bum): gnyen gyis yong su ma zin pa/sha stag 'byung ste/rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag ni blo chung zhing blo zhan par 'gyur ro//rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo gang shes pa rab kyi pha rol tu 
3a7('bum): phyin pa zab mo 'di mi 'dri /mi klog/lung mi 'bo ga/kha ton mi byed/mi sgom//tshul bzhin du yid la mi byed cig gzhan 'dri ba dang /klo ga pa dang /lung 'bogasapa dang /kha ton byed pa
3a8('bum): dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byid pa dag gi bar chad bye da pa de dag ni/blo shin tu hung bar 'gyur te/sangs rgyas kyi chos rlabs po che dag la de dag gi sems mi 'jug go/
3b1('bum): rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di 'dri ba dang /klog pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa dag la bdud kyi las 'di 
3b2('bum): dag kyang 'byung mod kyi/'on kyang rab 'byor rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo dag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa zab mo 'di 'dri zhing klog pa dang /lung 'bogacing /kha ton byed pa dang
3b3('bum): tshul bzhin du yid la byedacing /gong du bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs du rdzogs par byed pa dang/bzod pa'i 
3b4('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa yongs surdzogs par byed pa dang tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yogs su rdzogs par byed pa nga /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs sa rdzogs par byed pa dang gong du nang stong pa 
3b5('bum): nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed dang /phyi stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byad pa dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid yongs su rdzob par byed pa dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid yongs su rdzos par byed pa 
3b6('bum): dang|chen po stong pa nyid yongs su rdzongs par byed pa dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdzo gas par byed pa dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid yongs su rdzog par byed pa dang /'du ma byas stong pa 
3b7('bum): nyid yongs su rjogs par byed pa dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid yongs du rdzogs par bye ba dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid yongs surdzogs par byed dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid 
3b8('bum): yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang/rang bzhin stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogags par byed pa dang chos thams cad stong pa nyid yongs su rdzags par byed pa dang rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid yongs sa 
4a1('bum): @#//rdzogs par byed pa dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid yongs su rjogs par byed pa dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yongs 
4a2('bum): su rdzogs par byed pa dang/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yong su rdzogs par byed pa dang/gong du dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams yongs su rdzogs par byed ba dang /yang dag par spong pa rnams 
4a3('bum): yongs su rdzogs par byed padang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams yongs su rdzogs bar byed pa dang /dbang po rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed pa dang /stobs rnams yongs su rdzogs par
4a4('bum): byed pa dang /bsam gtan rnams yongs su rdzogs parabyed pa dang/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa yongs su rdzogs par byedapa dang /gong du 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams yongs su rdzogs
4a5('bum): byed pa dang/rnam par thar pa brgyad yongs su rdzogs par bye ba dang/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu yongs su rdzogs par byedab dang /rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /
4a6('bum): mtshan ma med ba dang/smon pa med pa yongs su rdzogs bar bye ba dang/mngon par shes ba rnams yongs su rdzogs parabyad pa dang/ting nge 'dzin rnams yongs su rdzogs ba byed ba dang /gzungs 
4a7('bum): kyi sgo rnams yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stongs rnams yongs su rdzogs bar byed pa dang/mi 'jigs pa rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed pa dang/so so yang dag par 
4b1('bum): rig pa rnams yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang /snying rje chen po yongs su rdzogs par byed dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams yongs su rdzogs bar byed pa dang /thams cad shes pa nyid 
4b2('bum): yongs su rdzogs par byed ba dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed cing /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su rdzogs bar byed/rab 'byor de ni sangs rgyas kyi mthu 
4b3('bum): yin par shes par bya ste/des rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di'i dra'o//klog go lung 'thog go//kha don byed do//tshul bzhin du yid la byed do//
4b4('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa zab mo 'di nyid yongs su rdzogs par bya do//gong du bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs 
4b5('bum): su rdzogs par byed do//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs du rdzogs par byed do//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yangs su rdzogs par byed do/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yogs su rdzogs par byed 
4b6('bum): do/gong du nang stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs byas par byed do//phyi stong pa nyid yongs su rdzob par byed do//phyi nang stong pa nyid yongs du rdzogs par 
4b7('bum): byed do//chen po stong pa nyid yongs su rdzongs par byed do//don dam pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdzo gas par byed do//'dus byas stong pa nyid yongs su rdzos par byed do//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid 
4b8('bum): yongs su rdzogs par byed do //mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdogs par byed do//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid yongs su brdzogs par byed do//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid 
5a1('bum): @#//yongs su rdzogs par byed do//rang bzhin stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed de//chos thams cad stong pa nyid yongs su rdzog par byed do//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa 
5a2('bum): nyid yongs su rdzogs par bya de//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdzo gas par byed do//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid yongs su rtogs par byed do//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed 
5a3('bum): do//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yo ngas su rdzogs par byed do //gong du dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams yongs su rdzogs par byed//yang dag par spong ba rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed do//

5a5('bum): par byed do//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa yongs su rdzogs par byed do//gong du 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams yongs su rdzogasapar byed do//bsam gtan rnams yongs su rdzogs pas 
5a6('bum): byed do//tshad med rnams yongs su rdzogs parabyed do//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams yongs su rdzogasabar byed do//rnam par thar pa brgyad yongs su rdzogs par byedado//mthar

5a8('bum): pa rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed do//ting nge 'dzin rnams yongs su rdzogs a par byed do//gzungs kyi sgo rnams yongs su rdzogs pa'i stobs rnams yongs su rdzogs

5b2('bum): pa rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed do//thams cad shes pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed de//lam gyi rnam par shes pa nyid yongs su rdzog par byed do//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su rdzogs bar 
5b3('bum): byed do//shar phyogs kyi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba de dag kyang rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag gis/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba dang /

5b6('bum): tshul bzhin du

5b8('bum): rol tu phyin pa 'di 'drib dang /bklag pa dang lung 'bog pa dang /kha tan bya ba dang /bstan pa dang/tshul bzhin du yid la ba ba'i phyir dgongs pa mdzad/byang phyogs kyi sangs rgyas bcol ldan 'das 
5b9('bum): gang ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba de dag kyang /rigs gyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag gis shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba dang /bkag pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha tog bya ba dang /
6a1('bum): @#//bstan pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bya ba'i phyir/dgongs pa mdzad do//byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams kyi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho 
6a2('bum): skyong ba de dag kyang /rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba dang /bklag pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha ton bya ba dang /bstan pa dang /tshul bzhinadu yid 
6a3('bum): la bya ba'i phyir dgongs pa mdzad do//shar lho'i phyogs mtshams kyi sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das gang ji sted cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba de dag kyang /ri gas kyi bu dang /rigs gyi bu mo de dag gis 
6a4('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa 'di 'dri ba dang /bklag pa dang /lung 'bo ga pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /bstan pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bya bli pyi dgongs pa mdzad do//lho nub kyi phyogs 
6a5('bum): mtshams kyi sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das gang ji snyed ci ga bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong pa de dag kyang /rigs kyibu dang rigs kyi bu mo de dag gi shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pa 'di 'dri ba dang 
6a6('bum): bklag pa dang /lung 'bod pa dang /kha ton bya ba'i dang /bstan pa dang /tshal bzhin du yid la bya ba'i phyir dgongs ba mdzad do//'og gi phyogs kyi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang jisnyed cig 
6a7('bum): bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong bde dag kyang /rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo dedag gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba dang /bklag pa dang /lung 'phog pa dang /kha ton bya ba dang /bstan

6b1('bum): kyi bu mo de dag gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba dang /bklag pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang //bton bya ba dang /bstan pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bya ba'i phyir dgongs pa mdzad do//
6b2('bum): shar phyogs kyi byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po//phyir mi ldog pa gang ji snyed pa de dag kyang rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa 'di 'bri zhing klog pa dang /lung 
6b3('bum): 'bog pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /tshul bzhin duyid la byed pa de dag gi don du brtson par byed cing yongs su 'dzin par byed do//lho phyogs kyi byang chub sems pa' sems dpa' chen po 
6b4('bum): phyir mi ldog pa gang ji snyid pa de dag kyang /rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyis bu mo shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa 'di 'bri zhing klogs pa dang /lung 'bog pa/kha ton byed pa dang ston pa dang /tshul bzhinadu yid 
6b5('bum): la byed pa de dag gi donadu brtson par byed cing yong su 'dzin par byed do//nub phyogs kyi byang chub sems de dag sems dpa' chen po phyir mi ldog pa gang ji sted pa de dag kyang /rig kyi bu dang rigs kyi
6b6('bum): bu mo shes rab kyi pha roladu phyin pa 'di 'bri zhing zlog pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha tog byed pa dang ston pa dang/tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa de dag gis don du brtson par byed cang /yongs su 'dzin 
6b7('bum): pa byed do//byang phyogs kyi byang chub semasad pa' sems dpa' chen pophyir mi ldog pa gang ji snyed pa de dag kyang /rigasakyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri zhing klog pa dang /
6b8('bum): lung 'bog pa dang /khaton byed pa dang ston pa dag tshul bzhin du yid la bye dpa de dag gi don du brtson par byed cing yangs su 'dzin par byed do//byang shar kyi phyogs mtshams kyi byang chub sems dpA

7b1('bum): pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang //bton byed pa dang /stan pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa de dag gi don du brtson par byed cing /yongs su 'dzin pa byed do//steng gi phyogs kyi byang chub sems dpa' 
7b2('bum): sems dpa' chen po phyer me ldog pa gang ji snyed pa de dag kyang /rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri zhing klog pa dang /lung 'bag pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /ston pa dang /
7b3('bum): tshul bzhin du yid la byed de dag gi don du brtson bar byed cing yongs su 'dzin par byed do//rab 'byor 'di lta ste dper na/bud med la la zhig la bu lnga ram/bcu 'am/nyi shu 'am/sum cu 'am/
7b4('bum): bzhi bcu 'am lnga bcu 'aM/brgya 'aM/stong zhig yod na/de dag ni ma de ci nas kyang min bar bya ba'i phyir brtson par byed cing /bdag cag gi ma ba di ji ltar na nad med par 'gyur/bdag cag gi ma 'di la ji ltar 
7b5('bum): na bar chad du mi 'gyur/bdag cag gi ma'adi ji ltar na ring du 'tsho bar 'gyur/bdag cag gi mi 'di ring du 'tsho bar bya na ji ltar bya/bdag cag gi ma 'di lus la mi bde ba'i nyams med par bya ba ni 
7b6('bum): ji ltar na/'di ni bdag cag skyed skyed pa ste/srog gi sbyin bdag go zhes de dag ma de la rim gro legs par bya bas rim gro byed do//de dag ni 'di'i srog gi bar chad du gyur du 'ong ngo //lus nyam 
7b7('bum): chung bar gyur du 'ong ngo //lus la sha sbrang dang /sbrang bu dang /sbrul gyis gtse ba'i mi bde bas bab du 'ong ngo //tsha ba dang /grang ba dang /bkres pa dang /skom pas mi bde bar gyur du 'ong ngo zhes phu dud 
7b8('bum): legs par bya bas phu dud du byed do//de ltar bu de dag ma de la 'di ni bdag cag skyed skye ba'o//'di ni 'jig rten 'di ston pa'o zhes bde ba'i yo byad thams cad kyis rgyun tu rim gro byed cing bsti 
8a1('bum): @#//stang dang /bkur sti cher byed do//rab 'byor de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
8a2('bum): phyin pa 'di la/sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par gzigs shing dgongs te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni bdag cag gi sangs rgyas kyi 
8a3('bum): chos thams cad skyed skyed pa'o//'jig rten 'di ston ston pa'o zhes dgongs so//shar phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra pa bcom 
8a4('bum): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'is sangs rgyas gang ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba dang /chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag kyang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa zab mo 'di la sangs 
8a5('bum): rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyu ni mi 'chad par gzigs shig dgongs so//lho phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi kha mas gang+ga'i klung gi bye masnyed dag na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
8a6('bum): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang ji snyed gcig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyongs pa dang chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das de dag yang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la 
8a7('bum): sangs rgyas gang ji snyed ci gzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba dag/chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas gcod ldan 'das de dag kyang /shes rab kyi pha rol? tu phyin pa zab mo 'dil sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis 
8b1('bum): rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par gzigs shing dgongs so//byang phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang 
8b2('bum): ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba dang /chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag kyang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bzigs shing dgongs so//lho 
8b3('bum): shing dgongs so//byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams bang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed du gan/de bzhin gshegs pa bgrab com pa yang dag par rdzog pa'i sangs rgyas gang ji snyed cig bzhugs 
8b4('bum): shing 'tsho skyong ba dang /tshes ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das de dag kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa zab mo 'di la sangs rgya kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun ma 'chad par gzigs shing dgongs so//
8b5('bum): shar lho'i phyogs mtshams kyi 'jig ten gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang ji snyid cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong 
8b6('bum): ba dang /chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag kyang/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na bzab mo 'di la/sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par gzigs shing dogangs so//lho 
8b7('bum): nub kyi phyogs mtshams kyi 'jig rtan gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gti bye ma snyed dag na/de 'azhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sengs rgyas gang ji snyed cig gzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba dang 
8b8('bum): chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das de dag gyad/shes rab kyi mdzes tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis brtag tu rgyun mi 'chad pa gzigs shing dgongs so//nub byang gi phyogs 
9a1('bum): @#//mtshams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang ji snyed cig bzhugs shing 'tsho 
9a2('bum): skyong ba dang /chos ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag kyang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par gzigs shing dgongs so//'og 
9a3('bum): gi phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams kang+ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed da ga na/de bzhin gshegs ba dgra bcom pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang ji snyed cig bzhugs shin 'tsho skyong ba dang /chos 
9a4('bum): ston pa'i sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das de dag kyang /shis rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa zab mo 'di la sangs cas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyu na mi 'chad bar gzigs shing dgongs so so//steng gi phyogs kyi 
9a5('bum): 'jig rten gyi khams gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rjogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang 'dzin snyed cig bzhugs shing dgongs so//steng gi phyogs kyi  
9a6('bum): rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dang kyang /shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa zab mo 'dil sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis stag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bzigs shing dgongs so/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ni de bzhin gshe 
9a7('bum): ba dgra bcom pa yang dgapar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams skyed skyed pa'o//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi ye shes 'di yang ston ston pa'o//de bas na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa  
9a8('bum): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyasarnams/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin bzab mo 'di la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad bar gzigs shing dgongs sa so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di
9b1('bum): las byung ba ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di las byung ba ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//
9b2('bum): 'di las byung ba ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'idi las byung ba ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di las byung ba ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o //'di las byung ba ni nang stong nyid do//'di las byung ni phyi stong 
9b3('bum): pa nyid do//'di las byung ba ni phyi nang stong pa nyid do//'di las byungab ni stong pa nyid stong pa nyid do//'di la byung ba ni chen po stong pa nyid do//'di lar byung ba ni don dam pa stong pa nyid do//'di lam byung ba ni 'dus 
9b4('bum): byas stong pa nyid do//'di la'i byung ba ni 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid do//'i da las byung ba ni mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid do//'i de las byung ba ni theg ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid do//'a de las byung ba ni 
9b5('bum): dor ba med pa stong pa nyid do//'di las byung ba ni rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//'di la'ang byung ba ni chos thams cad stong pa nyid do//'dilas byung ba ni rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid do//'i 'di las byung ba ni 
9b6('bum): mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid do//'dri las byung ba ni dngos po med pa stong pa nyid do/'di las byung ba ni ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//'od las byung ba ni dngos por med pa'i ngo bo nyid do//'di las byung ba ni 
9b7('bum): dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams so//'di las byung ba ni yang dag par spang ba rnams so//'di las byungab ni rdzu 'phrul kyi rkang pa rnams so//'di las byang ba ni dbang po rnams so//
9b8('bum): 'di las byung ba ni stobs rnams so//'di las byung/ba ni byang chub kyi yan lag rnams so//'di las byung:ba ni 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'o//'di las byung ba ni 'phags pa'i bde ba rnams so//
10a1('bum): @#//'di las byung ba ni bsam gtan rnams so//'di las byung ba ni tshad med pa rnams so//'di las byungab ni gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams so//'di las
10a2('bum): byung ba ni rnam par thar pa brgyad do//'di las byung ba ni mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug ba dgu'o//'di las byung ba ni rnam par thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid dang/mtshan ma med pa dang/smon pa med 
10a3('bum): pa'o//'di las byung ba ni ngon par shes pa rnams so//'di las byang ba ni ting nge 'dzin rnams so//'di las gyung ba ni gzung kyi sgo rnams so//'di las byungang ba ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams 
10a4('bum): soH'di las byung ba ni mi 'jigs pa rnams so//'di la byung ba ni so so yang dag par rig pa rnams so//'di las phyung ba ni snying rje chen po 'o      //'di las byung ba ni sangs rgyas kyi   
10a5('bum): chos ma 'dres pa rnams so//'di lasabyung ba ni rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'o//'di las byung ba ni lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//'di las byang ba ni phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o/'di las 
10a6('bum): byung ba ni dgra bcom pa nyid do//'di las byung ba ni rang byang chub po//'di las byung ba ni lam gyi rnam par shes pa nyid do//'di las byang ba ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub  
10a7('bum): por 'di las byung ba ni rgyun tu zhugs ba rnams so//'di las byu nga ba ni lan cig phyir 'od ba rnams so//'di las gyung ba ni phyir mi 'ong ba rnams so//'di las byang ba ni dgra bcom pa rnams so//
10a8('bum): 'di las byung ba ni nga sangs rgyas rnams so//'di las byung ba ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams so//'di las byungab ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs
10b1('bum): rgyas rnams te/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o//de dag thams cad ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la brten te bla na med pa yang dag 
10b2('bum): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub du mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas so//mngon par rdzogs par 'tshar rgya'o//mngon par rdzogs par tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro//rab 'byor byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa 
10b3('bum): pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa zab mo 'di 'dri zhing klog pa dang /lung 'bog pa dang /kha ton byed pa dang /sgom pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa de dag thams 
10b4('bum): cad kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par gzigs shing dgongs so//rab 'byor byang chub kyi sems dpa'i theg pa ba'i 
10b5('bum): rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo de dag la ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra btsom pa yang dag par rdzogasapa'i sangs rgyas rnams ci nas kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las yongs su 
10b6('bum): nyams par mi 'gyur bar rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad pa bsrung ba mdzad do//rab 'byor gyis gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das kyis gang 'di skad du/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni/de bzhin gshegs 
10b7('bum): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams bskyed pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na ba ni 'jig rten ston pa'o zhes bka' stsal pa/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na/shes rab kyi pha 
10b8('bum): rol tu phyin pa de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas rnams skyed pa lags/bcom ldan 'das ji lta ba na/shes rab kyi pha 
11a1('bum): @#//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas de bzhin gshegs pa bskrun pa lags/'jig rten ni de bzhin gshegs pas gang ma gsungs/bcom 
11a2('bum): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi rna ba 'dis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu skyed do//mi 'jigs pa bzhi bskyed do//so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi bskyed 
11a3('bum): do|byams pa chen po bskyed do//snying rje chen po bskyed do//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad bskyed do//thams cad shes pa nyid bskyed do//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid bskyed do//rnam 
11a4('bum): pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid bskyed do//rab 'byor chos de dag gis ni de bzhin gshegs par brjod do//ma byon pas na/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa zab mo 'dis/de bzhin gshegs 
11a5('bum): pa bskyed do//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i 'jig rten gang lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/rab 'byor phung po lnga ni de bzhin gshegs 
11a6('bum): pas 'jig rten du gsungs so//gsol pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mos phung po lnga ni ji ltar ston pa lags/bcom ldan 'das shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni ji 
11a7('bum): ltar phung po ston pa lags/bcom ldana'adas kyis bka' stsal pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa ni phung po lnga po 'di dag 'jig par ston pa ma yin/rab tu 'jig par ston pa ma yin te/
11a8('bum): skye zhes bya bar mi ston to//'gag ces bya bar mi ston to//kun nas nyon mongs shes bya bar mi ston to//rnam par byang shes bya bar mi ston to/'phel ces bya bar mi ston to/'grib ces bya bar 
11b1('bum): mi ston to//blang zhes bya bar mi ston to//dor zhes bya bar mi ston to/'das shes bya bar mi ston to/ma 'ongs shes bya bar mi ston to//da ltar byung zhes bya bar mi ston to //de ci'i phyir zhe na/stong pa 
11b2('bum): nyid la 'jig pa med do//rab tu 'jig pa med do//mtshan ma med pa la 'jig pa med do/rab tu 'jig pa med do //smon pa med pa la 'jig pa med de//rab tu 'jig pa med//mngon par 
11b3('bum): 'du bya ba med pa la 'jig pa'am/rab tu 'jig pa med de//skye ba med pa la 'jig pa 'am rab tu 'jig pa med do//dngos po med pa la 'jig pa'am rab tu 'jig pa med/dang po pa med pa la 'jig pa'am 
11b4('bum): rab tu 'jig pa med do/ /rab 'byor de ltar de bzhin gshegs pas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'jig rten ston par gsungs te/rab 'byor sems can tshad med grangs med dpag tu 
11b5('bum): med pa'i sems dang spyod pa mkhyen pa de dag kyang /shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di la brten cing rab tu mkhyen te/rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa zab mo 'di la ni sems can med do//sems 
11b6('bum): can du gdags pa mi dmigs so//gzugs med do//gzugs su gdags pa mi dmigs so //tshor ba med do//tshor bar gdags pa mi dmigs so//'du shes med do//'du shes su gdags pa 
11b7('bum): mi dmigs so//'du byed med do//'du byed du gdags pa mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa med do//rnam par shes par gdags pa mi dmigs sa so//mig med do//mig tu gdags pa mi   
11b8('bum): dmigs so//rna ba med do//rna bar gdags pa mi dmig sa so//sna med do//snar gdags pa mi dmigs so//lce med do//lcer gdags pa mi dmigs se//lus med do//lus su gdags 
12a1('bum): pa mi dmigs so//yid med do//yid du gdags pa mi dmigs so//gzugs med do//gzugs lug dgas pa mi dmigs so/sgra med do//sgrar gdags pa mi dmigs 
12a2('bum): dri med do//drir gdags pa mi dmigs so//ro med do//ror gdags pa mi dmigs sa//reg med do//reg tu gdags pa mi dmigs so/
12b1('bum): rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med do//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar gdags pa mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyes tshor ba med do//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i
12b2('bum): rkyen gyis tshor bar gdags ba mi dmigs so//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba med do//lce'i 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba gdags pa mi dmigs so so//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i
12b3('bum): rkyen kyis tshor ba med do//lus kyi 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar gdagasapa mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba med do///yid kyi 'dus te regapa'i rkyenagyis tshor bar
12b4('bum): gdags pa mi dmigs so//sa'i khams med do//sa'i khams su gdags pa mi dmigs so/chu'i khams med do//chu'i khams su gdags pa ming dmigs so//me'i khams med do//me'i khams
12b5('bum): su gdags pa mi dmigs sa//rlung gi khams med do//rlung gi khams su gdags pa mi migs so//nam mkha'i khams med do//nam mkha'i khams su gdags pa midamigs so//rnam par
12b6('bum): shes pa'i khams med do//rnam par shes pa'i khams su gdags pa migs so//ma rig pa med do//ma rig par gdags pa mi dmigs so//'du byed med do//'du byed du gdags par mi
12b7('bum): dmigs so//rnam par shes pa med do//rnam par shes bar gdags pa mi dmigs lo//ming dang gzugs med do///ming dang gzugs su gdags pa mi dmigasaso//skye mched drug med do//
12b8('bum): skye mched drug tu gdags pa mi dmigs sog/reg pa med do//reg par gdags pa ming migs sog/tshor ba med do//tshor bar gdags pa mi dmig sa so//sred pa med do//sred par gdags pa
13a1('bum): @#//mi dmigs so//len pa med do//len par gdags pa mi dmigs so//srid pa med do//srid par gdags pa mi dmigs//skye ba med do//skye bar gdags 
13a2('bum): pa mi dmigs so//rga shi med do//rga shir gdags pa mi dmigs sa so//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa med do//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu byin par gdags pa mi dmig sa so//tshul khrims kyi 
13a3('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa med do //tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin par gdags pa mi dmi gas so//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa mid do//bzod pa'i pha rol du phyin par gdags pa mi  dmigs so//
13a4('bum): brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa med de//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin par ga dgas pa mi dmigs so/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa med to//bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin par 
13a5('bum): gdags pa mi dmigs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa me da do//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na par gdags pa mi dmigs so//nang stong pa nyid med do//nang stong ba nyid du gdags pa mi dmigs 
13a6('bum): so/phyi stong pa nyid med do/phyi stong pa nyid du gdags par mi dmigs so//phyi nang stong pa nyid med do//phyi nang stong pa nyid du gdags 
13a7('bum): par mi dmigs so//stong pa nyid du gdags pa mi dmyigs so//chen po stong pa nyid med do/chen po stong pa nyid du gdags ba mi dmigs so//don dam pa stong pa nyid med do/don dam pa stong pa nyid du gdags la mi dmigs 
13a8('bum): so/'dus byas stong pa nyid med do/'dus byas stong pa nyid du gdags pa'i mi dmigs so//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid med de/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid du gdag pa mi dmigs so//mtha' las 
13b1('bum): 'das pa stong pa nyid med do//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid du gdags pa mi dmigs so//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid med do//thog ma dang tha ma med pa steng pa nyid du gdags pa med migs 
13b2('bum): so//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid med/dor ba med pa stong pa nid du gdags pa mi dmigs/rang bzhin stong pa nyid med do/rang bzhin stong pa nyid du gdags 
13b3('bum): pa mi dmigs so//chos thams cad stong pa nyid med do/chos thams cad stong pa nyi du gdags pa mi dmigs sa//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid med do//rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid du gdags pa mi 
13b4('bum): dmigs so//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid med de//mi dmigs pa stong pa'i nyid du gdags pa mi dmi gas so//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid med de do//dngos po med pa stong ba nyid du gdags 
13b5('bum): pa mi mi dmigs so//bying chub kyi yan lag rnams med do//byang chub kyi yan lag tu gdags pa mi dmigs so//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa med do//'phags pa'i lam yan
14a1('bum): @#//lag brgyad par gdags pa mi dmigs so//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams med do//'phags pa'i bden par gdags pa mi dmigs//bsam gtan med do//
14a2('bum): bsam gtan du gdags pa mi dmigs so //tshad med pa rnams med do//tshad med par gdags pa mi dmi gas so//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams med do//gzugs med 
14a3('bum): pa'i snyoms par 'jug par gdags pa mi dmigasaso//rnam par thar pa brgyad med do//rnam par thar pa brgyad/du gdags pa mi dmigs //mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug 
14a4('bum): pa dgu med do//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyims par 'jug pa dgur gdags pa mi dmigs so//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa med do//rnam par
14a5('bum): thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dag/mtshan ma med pa dang/smon pa med par gdags pa mi dmigs so//mngon par shes pa rnams med do//mngon par shes par gdags pa mi dmigs so//ting nge 
14a6('bum): 'dzin rnams med do//ting nge 'dzin du gdags pa mi dmigs so //gzungs kyi sgo rnams med do//gzungs kyi sgor gdags pa mi dmigs sa //de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams med do//
14a7('bum): bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs su gdags pa mi dmigs so//mi 'jigs pa rnams med do//mi 'jigs par gdags pa mi dmi gas so//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams med do//so 
14a8('bum): bar dag par rig par gdags pa mi dmigasaso//snying rje chen po med do//snying rje chen por gdags pa mi dmigs so//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams med do//sangs rgyas kyi 
14b1('bum): chos ma 'dres par gdags pa mi dmigs so//thams cad shes pa nyid med do//thams cad shes pa nying du gdags pa mi dmigs so//lam gyi rnam par shes pa nyid med do/lam gyi rnam par 
14b2('bum): shes pa nyid du gdags pa mi dmigs swo//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid med do//rnam pa thams cad mkhyes pa nyid du gdags pa yin pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi 'jig rten ston pa yin te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di ni/gzugs ston par byed pa ma yin no//tshong bston par byed pa ma yin 
14b3('bum): no//'du shes ston par byed pa ma yin//'du byed ston par byed pa ma yin na//rnam par shes pa ston par byed pa ma yin pa ni//mig ston par byed pa ma yin te//rna ba ston par byed pa ma yin zhe na//sna ston 
14b4('bum): par byed pa ma yin no//lce ston par byed pa ma yin to//lus ston par byed pa ma yi na no//yid ston par byed pa ma yinaH/gzugs ston par byed pa ni//sgra ston par byed pa ma yin)ni//dri 
14b5('bum): ston par byed pa ma yin gyi don//ro ston par byed pa ma yin la//rig ston par byed pa ma yin//chos ston par byed pa ma yin
14b6('bum): zhes pa ston par byed pa ma ma yin no//sna'i rnam par shes pa ston parbyed pa ma yin no//lce'i rnam par shes pa ston2 byed pa ma yin no//lus kyi rnam par shes pa ston pa byed pa ma yin
14b7('bum): na//yid kyi rnam par shes pa ston bar byed pa ma yin no/mig gi 'dus te reg pa ston par byed pa ma yid do//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa ston bar byed pa ma yang yin 
15a1('bum): @#//lce'i 'dus te reg pa ston par byed pa ma yin no//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa ston pa byed pa ma yin no//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa ston na byed pa ma yin no//mig gi 'dus 
15a2('bum): reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ston par byed pa ma yin//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bston par byed pa ma yin na//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba ston par byed ma yin na/
15a3('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gis tshor bston par byed dam ma yin no//lus kyi 'dis te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tsher bston par byed pa ma yi na//yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor bston pa 
15a4('bum): byed pa ma yin no//sa'i khams ston par byed pa ma yin pa ni//chu'i khams ston par byed ba ma yin no//me'i khams ston par byed pam yin no//rlung gi khams ston par byed pa mi yin no//
15a5('bum): nam mkha'i khams ston par byed pa'i ma yin no//rnam par shes pa'i khams ston par byed pa yin no//ma rig pa ston par byed pa ma yin no//'du byed ston par byed pa ma yin ni//rnam par 
15a6('bum): shes pa ston par byed pa ma ma yin no//ming dang gzugs ston par byed pa ma yin//skye mched drug ston par byed pa ma yin na//reg pa ston par byed pa ma zhe na//tshor ba ston par byed pa

28a1('bum): @#//bu dag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di ni mtshan nyid do//lha'i bu dag shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa zab mo 'di ni smon pa med pa'i mtshan

53a3('bum): byams pa chen po'i 'jig rten dben zhes bye bar ston to//snying rje chen po'i 'jig rten dpe na zhes bye bar ston to/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi 'jig rten dben zhe bya bar 
53a4('bum): ston to//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'i 'jig rten dben zhes ba bar ston to//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'i 'jig rten de ben zhes bya bar ston te/phyir me 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'i 'jigs rten dben 
53a5('bum): zhes bya bar ston to//dgra bcom pa nyid kyi 'jig rten dben zhis bya bar ston to//rang byang chub kyi 'jig rten dben zhes bya bar ston to/lam gyi rnam par shes pa nyid kyi 'jig rten dpe na zhe bya bar 
53a6('bum): ston to/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi 'jig rten dang ben zhes bya bar ston to /rab 'byor de ltar na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa de bzhin gshegs pa skyed cing 'jig rten ston to//rab 'byor gzhan

53b2('bum): pa stong pa zhes bya bar stong to//rna ba'i 'jig rten mtha' las mdas pa stong pa zhes bya ba ston to//sna'i 'jig rten mtha' las byung ba stong pa zhes bya bar stonato//lce'i 'jig rten mtha' las gdas 
53b3('bum): pa stong pa zhes bya ba stonato//lus kyi 'jig rten mtha' lasa'adas pa stong pa zhes bya par stonato//yid kyi 'jig rten mtha' yas 'das pa stong pa zhes ba bar stonato//gzugs kyi 'jig

59b1('bum): bya bar ston to//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams kyi 'jig rten ngo bo nyid stong pa zhes bya bar ston to//byams pa chen po'i 'jig rten ngo bo nyid stongab zhes bya bar

316b7('bum): tshor ba'o//bdag ni lus kyi 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba kunatshor ba'o//bdag gi yid kyi 'dusata reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba'o//bdag ni yid kyi 'das te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba kun tshor 
316b8('bum): ba'o//bdag gi sa'i khams so//bdag ni sa'i khams las rnam par ma 'das pa'o//bdag gi chu'i khams so//bdag ni chu'i khams las rnam par ma'adas pa'o//bdag gi me'i khams so//
317a1('bum): @#//bdag ni me'i khams las rnam par ma 'das pa'o//bdag gi rlung gi khams so//bdag ni rlung gi khams las rnam par ma 'dus pa'o//bdag gi nam mkha'i khams so//bdag ni 
317a2('bum): nam mkha'i khams las rnam par ma bdas pa'o//bdag gi rnam par shes pa'i khams so//bdag ni rnam par shes pa'i khams las rnam par mdzad pa'o//bdag gi ma rig pa'o//bdag ni 
317a3('bum): ma rig pa can no//bdag gi 'du byed do//bdag ni 'du byed pa'o//bdag gi rnam parashes pa'o//bdag ni rnam parashes par byed pa'o//bdag gi ming dang gzugs so//bdag ni ming dang gzugs 
317a4('bum): can no//bdag gi skye mched drug go/bdag ni skye mched drug can no//bdag gi reg pa'o//bdag ni reg par byed pa'o//bdag ni tshor ba'o//bdag ni tshor bar byed pa'o//bdag gi sred pa'o//
317a5('bum): bdag ni sred par byed pa'o//bdag ga len pa'o//bdag ni len pa can no//bdag gi srid pa'o//bdag ni srid pa can no//bdag gi skye ba'o//bdag ni skye ba'i chos can no//bdag gi rga shi'o//
317a6('bum): bdag ni rga shi'i chos can no//bdag ga'i sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bdag gis sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyinapa yongs su rdzogs par bya'o//bdag gi tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//

317a8('bum): gi brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o////bdag gis brtson 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par ba ba'o//bdag gi bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/bdag gis bsam gtan 
317b1('bum): gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par bya'o//bdag gi shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bdag gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa yongs su rdzogs par bye'o//bdag gi nang stong pa nyid do//
317b2('bum): bdag gis nang stong pa nyid la goms par bya'o//bdag gis phyi stong pa nyid do//bdag gis phyi stong ba nyid la goms par bya'o/bdag gis phyi nang stong pa nyid do//bdag gyis phyi nang stong pa nyid la 
317b3('bum): goms par bya'o//bdag dag gi stong pa nyid stong pa nyid do//bdag dag gis stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la goms pa ra bya'o//bdag gi chen po stong pa nyid do//bdag gas chen po stong pa nyid la goms pa'i 
317b4('bum): bya'o//bdag gi don dam pa stong pa nyid do//bdag gos don dam pa stong pa nyidal goms par bya'o//bad ga gi 'dus byas stong pa nyid do//bdagag gis 'dus byas stong pa nyid la goms pas bya'o//
317b5('bum): bdag gi 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid do/bdag gis 'dus ma byas stong pa nyi da la goms par bya'o//padag gi mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid do//ba dag gis mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid 
317b6('bum): la goms par bya'o//bdagag gi thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid do//bag gis thog ma dang tha ma med ba stong pa nyid la goms bar bya'o//bdag gi dor ba med pa stong pa nyid do//bdag gis 
317b7('bum): dor ba med pa stong ba nyid la goms par bya'o/bdag gi rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//bdagagis rang bzhin stong pa nyid la goms par bya'o//bdag gi chos thams cad stong pa nyid do//bdagis 
317b8('bum): chos thams cad stong pa nyi da la goms bar bya'o/bdag gi rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid do//bad ga gis rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid la goms bar bya'o//bdag gi mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid 
318a1('bum): @#//bdag gis mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid la goms par bya'o//bdag gi dngos po med pa stong pa nyid do//bdag gis dngos po med pa stong pa yid la goms par bya'o//ba dag gi ngo bo 
318a2('bum): nyid stong pa nyid do//bdag gas ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la goms bar bya'o//bdag gi dngos pa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//ba dag gis dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong ba nyid la goms 
318a3('bum): bar bya'o//bdag gi dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams so//bdag gis dran pa nye bar gzhag pa nams la spyad par bya'o//bdag gi yang dag par spong ba rnams so//bdag gis yang dag par spong ba
318a4('bum): rnams la spyad par bya'o//ba dag gi rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams so//bdag gi rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang ba rnams la spyad par bya'o/bdag gi dbang po rnams so//bdag gis dbang po rnams la spyad 
318a5('bum): par bya'o//bdag gi stobs rnams so//bdag gis stobs rnams la spyad par bya'o/ bdag gi byang chub kyi yan lag rnams so//bdag gis byang chub kyi yan lag rnam la spyad par bya'o//bdag

318b3('bum): pa med pa la spyad par bya'o//bdagag gi mngon par shes pa rnams so//bdag gi mngon par shes pa rnalas la spyad par bya'o//ba dag gi ting nge 'dzin rnams so//bdag gis ting nge 
318b4('bum): 'dzin rnams la spyad par bya'o/bdag gi gzungs kyi sgo rnams so//bdag gis gzungs kyi sgo rnams la spyad par bya'o//bdag gi de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams so//
318b5('bum): bdag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i stong pa rnams la spyad par bya/bdag gi mi 'jigs pa rnams so//bdag gas mi 'jigs pa rnams la spyad par bya'o/ /bdag gi so so 
318b6('bum): yang dag par rig pa rnams so//bdag gis so so yang dag par rig pa rnam la spyad par bya'o//bdag gi byams pa chen po'o//bdag gis byams pa chen po la spyad par bya'o//
318b7('bum): bdag gi snying rje chen po'o//bdag gis snying rje chen po la spyad par bya'o/ /bad ga gi sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams so//bdag gyis sangs rgyas kyi chos ma
319a1('bum): @#//'dres pa rnams la spyad par bya'o//bdag gi rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'o//bdag ni rgyun tu zhugs par 'gyur ro//bdag gi lan cig phyir 
319a2('bum): 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//bdag ni lan cig phyir 'ong bar 'gyur ro//bdag gi phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//bad ga ni phyir mi 'ong bar 'gyur ro//ba dag gi
